Therefore, we introduce H2R-Bench, a benchmark for evaluating cross-embodiment human-to-robot manipulation video generation, where models transform egocentric human demonstrations into robot manipulation videos under specified embodiments. Each benchmark instance contains a human demonstration video, target embodiment constraints, and source-grounded annotations covering task goals, action events, functional contacts, and object responses. H2R-Bench evaluates generated videos through five dimensions, including goal-state completion, action-event completion, functional contact transfer, embodiment correctness, and general video quality. We benchmark eleven state-of-the-art video generation models across six manipulation families and two robot embodiments. Our evaluation reveals that current video world models remain limited in human-to-robot manipulation transfer: even leading models often fail in embodiment consistency, functional interaction, and task execution. H2R-Bench provides a systematic diagnostic framework for evaluating whether video world models can bridge the human-to-robot embodiment gap and convert human manipulation observations into robot-centric training resources.

</details>


### [104] [Semantic Radiance Fields as Simulators for Spatial Reasoning in Real-World Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13095)
*Nico Heider,Michał Jan Włodarczyk,Katarzyna Wasielewska-Michniewska,Przemysław Hołda,Martin Schieck,Marcin Paprzycki,Maria Ganzha,Bogdan Franczyk*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出使用语义辐射场（SRF）作为具身智能体空间推理的仿真器，通过将预训练视觉模型的2D语义分割提升到3D辐射场中，同时编码几何、外观和逐类语义身份，从而兼顾真实感与地面真值语义，实现从真实场景重建的多样化环境训练与评估空间推理模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在具身智能体中训练和评估空间推理能力需要既在几何上忠实又具备语义可查询性的多样化环境。现有合成仿真器虽然提供地面真值语义，但牺牲了真实感；基于真实世界重建的仿真器虽具有逼真外观，但缺乏默认的地面真值语义。需要一种统一两种优势的表征。

Method: 提出使用语义辐射场（SRF）作为空间推理智能体的仿真器。SRF将预训练视觉模型产生的2D语义分割提升到3D辐射场中，联合编码几何、外观和逐类语义身份。该场从真实场景的有位姿RGB图像重建，支持新视角合成、语义查询和自由空间查询。作为示例，构建了果园苹果采摘任务的SRF驱动仿真器，辐射场为物理引擎提供相机渲染、语义地面真值和占用查询。

Result: 论文概述了SRF驱动的仿真器框架，以果园苹果采摘任务作为示例应用，说明该方法能够高效生成多样化真实世界环境用于训练和评估空间推理模型，但摘要中未给出具体的定量实验结果。

Conclusion: 语义辐射场作为仿真器是一种有前景的方案，能够统一真实感外观与语义地面真值，为具身智能体的空间推理训练和评估提供高效且多样化的真实世界环境。

Abstract: Training and evaluating spatial reasoning in embodied agents requires diverse environments that are both geometrically faithful and semantically queryable. Synthetic simulators offer ground truth semantics but sacrifice realism; simulators based on reconstructions of real-world environments have realistic appearance but lack ground truth semantics by default. We propose using Semantic Radiance Fields (SRF) as simulators for spatial reasoning agents. SRFs are a representation that unifies these requirements by lifting 2D semantic segmentations from pretrained vision models into a 3D radiance field that jointly encodes geometry, appearance, and per-class semantic identity. The resulting fields are reconstructed from posed RGB captures of real scenes and support novel-view synthesis, semantic and free-space queries within a single grounded representation. This enables the efficient generation of diverse real-world environments to train and evaluate spatial reasoning models. As an example application, we outline an SRF-driven simulator for an orchard apple-reaching task, in which the radiance field supplies camera rendering, semantic ground truth, and occupancy queries to a physics engine.

</details>


### [105] [S2-HWM: Sparse Event-Structured Hierarchical World Model for Long-Horizon Surgical Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13103)
*Shuzhe Zhang,Xin Zhu,Yinling Qian,Qiong Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出S2-HWM（稀疏事件结构化层次世界模型），通过从原始隐轨迹中学习稀疏事件证据，协调事件级管理层与原始步执行层，解决了长时程手术机器人操作中奖励稀疏、交互变化间隔不规则的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程手术机器人操作面临奖励稀疏和交互变化间隔不规则的挑战；现有世界模型以原始步分辨率想象任务进展，缺乏对任务阶段性进度的显式建模；手动划分阶段难以与状态依赖的交互转换对齐。

Method: S2-HWM从原始隐轨迹中学习稀疏事件证据，用于调度管理层的目标更新，并分割出可变时长的片段供事件转换模型（ETM）预测边界随机状态、片段时长和累计奖励；通过链式事件级预测实现超出原始想象范围的变时长续推，同时执行层保留原始步actor-critic学习。

Result: 在基于SurRoL的PegTransfer任务上，S2-HWM成功率达98.7%，比扁平的GAS DreamerV3基线高出22.7个百分点。

Conclusion: S2-HWM通过引入事件级结构化层次世界模型，有效捕捉了变时长任务进度，大幅提升了手术机器人操作的成功率，验证了事件级抽象对长时程稀疏奖励任务的价值。

Abstract: Long-horizon surgical robot manipulation is challenging because task rewards are sparse, while meaningful interaction changes occur at irregular intervals. Existing world-model agents typically imagine at primitive-step resolution, leaving variable-duration task progress implicit. Manually specified stages can provide intermediate structure, but their task specific boundaries are difficult to align with state-dependent interaction transitions. We propose S2-HWM, a Sparse Event-Structured Hierarchical World Model that learns sparse event evidence from primitive latent trajectories to coordinate an event-level manager and a primitive-step worker. The event evidence schedules manager goal updates, and each selected latent goal conditions the worker's primitive actions until the next update. The learned event evidence also forms variable-duration segments for an Event Transition Model (ETM), which predicts the next?boundary stochastic state, segment duration, and accumulated segment reward. Chaining these event-level predictions provides a variable-duration continuation beyond the primitive imagination horizon for manager learning, while the worker retains primitive-step actor-critic learning. On a SurRoL-based PegTransfer task, S2-HWM achieves a success rate of 98.7%, outperforming the flat GAS DreamerV3 baseline by 22.7 percentage points.

</details>


### [106] [FAM-DQ: A Dual-Quadrotor-Based Fully Actuated Aerial Manipulator for High-Torque Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13220)
*Xuwei Yang,Ruoyu Ren,Ziqian Guo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出FAM-DQ，一种基于双四旋翼的全驱动空中机械臂平台，通过被动关节连接两个四旋翼推进模块并利用齿轮驱动伺服控制指向，实现6自由度解耦的末端执行器控制，具备全向操作能力与高扭矩输出。实验验证其最大扭矩达1.019 N·m，总质量仅0.447 kg，扭矩质量比达2.28 N·m/kg，有效解决了传统欠驱动空中机械臂位置-姿态强耦合及全驱动平台结构复杂、扭矩不足等问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的欠驱动空中机械臂存在位置-姿态强耦合问题，而全驱动平台设计面临结构复杂、载荷能力有限和扭矩输出不足的挑战，难以满足空中物理交互任务对大交互力与精确末端控制的需求。

Method: 采用双四旋翼结构，将两个四旋翼推进模块通过被动关节安装在中央框架两端，并利用齿轮驱动伺服调节指向方向，实现全驱动6自由度解耦控制与全向操作能力，同时提升扭矩输出。

Result: 实验验证了轨迹跟踪、姿态跟踪、静态扭矩测量和螺丝驱动等任务，最大扭矩达1.019 N·m，总质量0.447 kg，扭矩质量比2.28 N·m/kg。

Conclusion: FAM-DQ通过创新的双四旋翼全驱动构型，在保持结构简洁的同时实现了高扭矩输出与精确的6自由度末端控制，为空中物理交互任务提供了一种有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Aerial physical interaction requires aerial manipulation platforms to generate large interaction forces and torques while maintaining precise end-effector control. However, conventional underactuated aerial manipulators suffer from strong position-attitude coupling, whereas fully actuated platform designs often face structural complexity, limited payload capacity, and insufficient torque output. This paper presents FAM-DQ, a dual-quadrotor based fully actuated aerial manipulator designed for high-torque physical interaction tasks. By mounting two quadrotor propulsion modules at the ends of a central frame through passive joints, while using a gear-driven servo to regulate the pointing direction, FAM-DQ achieves decoupled $6$-DoF end-effector control with omnidirectional manipulation capability and enhanced torque output. Experiments including trajectory tracking, attitude tracking, static torque measurement, and screw driving validate the proposed design. FAM-DQ achieves a maximum torque of $1.019~\mathrm{N}\cdot\mathrm{m}$ with a total mass of $0.447~\mathrm{kg}$, corresponding to a torque-to-mass ratio of $2.28~\mathrm{N}\cdot\mathrm{m/kg}$.

</details>


### [107] [Manufacturing Complex Airtight Soft Pneumatic Actuators for Soft Robotics: Process Evaluation and Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13233)
*Mohammed Abboodi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文对多种软体气动执行器的制造工艺（热缩成型、硅胶浇铸、粉末/液体增材制造及FDM）进行了系统性评估，发现FDM是最具适应性的制造路线，并提出了以工艺-结构兼容性为核心的设计制造方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂的软体气动执行器需要同时满足几何保真度、柔顺性、结构完整性和气密性，而当前制造方法难以兼顾这些要求，缺乏系统的工艺选择指导。

Method: 采用工艺筛选、基线制造、失效分析和工艺改进四步评估框架，对热缩成型、硅胶浇铸、粉末基与液体基增材制造及FDM等制造路线进行系统比较，分析各工艺的固有局限与可修正缺陷。

Result: 热缩成型受限于几何贴合度，浇铸受限于模具可达性和粘接界面，粉末基方法受限于封闭通道内残留材料，DLP受限于材料性能和后处理要求；FDM因其主导缺陷可通过工艺优化逐步消除而成为最灵活的方案。气密性不仅取决于壁厚，还与挤出路径架构密切相关。

Conclusion: 建立了面向制造的软体执行器设计方法，强调工艺选择应基于执行器架构与制造约束之间的兼容性，为软体机器人中复杂、柔性且气密的执行器开发提供了实用指导。

Abstract: Manufacturing complex soft pneumatic actuators remains challenging because geometric fidelity, compliance, structural integrity, and airtightness must be achieved simultaneously. This study presents a manufacturing-focused evaluation of several fabrication routes for complex pneumatic structures, including heat-shrink forming, silicone casting, powder- and liquid-based additive manufacturing, and fused deposition modeling (FDM). The processes were assessed through process screening, baseline fabrication, failure analysis, and process improvement to distinguish inherent process limitations from correctable manufacturing defects. Heat-shrink forming was limited by geometric conformity, casting by mold accessibility and bonded interfaces, powder-based methods by residual material trapped within enclosed passages, and digital light processing by the material properties and post-processing requirements of the investigated system. FDM provided the most adaptable route because its dominant defects could be progressively reduced through process optimization. The results further showed that airtightness depends not only on nominal wall thickness but also on extrusion-path architecture, while support-free geometry is important when access for internal post-processing is limited. These findings establish a practical design-for-manufacturing approach in which process selection is guided by the compatibility between actuator architecture and manufacturing constraints. The proposed approach provides practical guidance for developing complex, flexible, and airtight soft pneumatic actuators for soft robotic applications

</details>


### [108] [Predictive Relative-Velocity Steering for Safe Robotic Manipulator Teleoperation in Dynamic Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13284)
*Changhao Hu,Zeyi Liu,Songqiao Hu,Shuang Liu,Zihan Meng,Xiao He*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种轻量级模块化的遥操作主动避碰框架，在末端执行器速度指令层面，通过基于碰撞时间(TTC)的预测和Rodrigues旋转公式对相对速度矢量进行偏转，在保持速度大小的同时改变方向，有效避免突然出现的障碍物，并缓解了传统APF方法的死锁问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 遥操作中操作员可能因网络延迟或注意力有限而无法及时有效地躲避突然出现的障碍物，造成安全风险。现有方法缺乏在速度指令层面对突然障碍进行主动预测与规避的轻量级方案。

Method: 该框架首先对点云进行预处理；然后基于带超调保护的碰撞时间(TTC)预测潜在碰撞；随后利用Rodrigues旋转公式旋转相对速度矢量，仅改变方向而不改变大小，从而避免传统人工势场(APF)方法的死锁问题；预测模块补偿了复杂遥操作流程中因点云处理带来的延迟。

Result: 在多种场景的仿真中，该方法相比基线方法实现了更高的末端执行器避碰率；在物理机器人系统上的实验也进一步验证了其避碰有效性。

Conclusion: 该轻量级设计支持遥操作所需的高频控制，能够在速度指令层面实现有效的主动避碰，兼顾了安全性、实时性与实用性。

Abstract: Recent advances in teleoperation have enabled robotic manipulators to perform dexterous, human-arm-like motions. However, human operators may fail to avoid suddenly appearing obstacles promptly and effectively, particularly under network latency or limited attention, thereby creating safety risks. To address this issue, we propose a lightweight and modular framework for proactive collision avoidance, operating directly at the end-effector velocity-command level. After preprocessing the point cloud, the framework first predicts potential collisions based on time-to-collision (TTC) with integrated overshoot protection, and subsequently rotates the relative-velocity vector using Rodrigues' rotation formula. The deflection changes only the direction of the relative velocity while preserving its magnitude, thereby mitigating the deadlock problem commonly encountered by conventional artificial potential field (APF) methods. The prediction module compensates for point-cloud processing latency introduced by complex teleoperation pipelines, while the lightweight design enables the high-frequency control required for teleoperation. Simulations across diverse scenarios show that the proposed method achieves a higher end-effector collision avoidance rate than the baseline methods. Experiments on a physical robotic system further validate its collision-avoidance effectiveness.

</details>


### [109] [NestDex: Nested Policy Learning with Copilot Assisted Teleoperation for Dexterous Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13362)
*James Zhao,Jinhe Tang,Mingyuan Ba,Weiming Zhi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: NestDex提出了一种嵌套策略学习框架，通过利用学习到的手部技能辅助演示数据采集，操作员只需控制手臂并通过单自由度离合器调节手部技能，从而降低灵巧操作的演示收集难度，并在真实世界实验中验证了其有效性和可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灵巧操作中的演示数据收集极其困难，因为操作员需要同时协调手臂运动与精确的、接触丰富的手指行为。与平行钳爪操作不同，灵巧任务全程要求手指的精细控制，直接指定完整手指轨迹的演示方式负担过重，限制了灵巧操作学习的发展。

Method: NestDex采用嵌套策略框架：操作员控制手臂并通过单自由度离合器调节内层手部技能，内层手策略根据最新的本体感觉历史自适应调整动作；同时使用视觉-语言选择器为每个任务阶段激活合适的技能。收集到的演示用于训练独立的外层视觉运动策略，部署时可同时控制手臂和手而不依赖内层策略。手部动作通过变分自编码器提供紧凑的动作目标，同时保留手臂关节空间指令。

Result: 在多项真实世界灵巧操作实验中，NestDex提高了演示数据采集的可靠性和效率，实证评估支持了有效的自主策略学习。

Conclusion: NestDex通过嵌套策略设计显著减轻了灵巧操作演示收集的负担，实现了高质量的自主策略学习，为灵巧操作领域提供了更实用的数据采集与策略学习方案。

Abstract: Dexterous manipulation promises substantially richer robot interaction with the physical world, but learning these behaviours remains constrained by the difficulty of collecting consistent, complete-task demonstrations. Unlike parallel-jaw manipulation, dexterous tasks require the operator to coordinate arm motion with precise, contact-rich finger behaviour throughout the task. We introduce NestDex, a nested policy-learning framework that reduces this burden by using learned hand skills to assist demonstration collection. The operator controls the arm and regulates the active hand skill through a single-DoF clutch, rather than directly specifying the full finger trajectory. The inner hand policy adapts its motion from the latest proprioceptive history, while a vision-language selector activates the appropriate skill for each task stage. The resulting demonstrations train a separate outer visuomotor policy that controls both the arm and hand without the inner policies at deployment. A hand-action variational autoencoder provides compact hand-action targets while retaining arm commands in joint space. Across real-world dexterous manipulation experiments, NestDex improves demonstration reliability and efficiency, and the resulting empirical evaluations support effective autonomous policy learning. Video Demo are available at project website https://aus.bot/research/nestdex.

</details>


### [110] [FIRE-VLA: Failure-Informed Self-Evolution for Vision-Language-Action Models in Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13395)
*Hao Dou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出FIRE-VLA，一种失败感知的自进化框架，用于改进自动驾驶视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型的强化学习训练。当GRPO采样的轨迹组全部为低质量时，FIRE-VLA会从未解决的失败中提取信息，通过从冻结的回合初始模型副本进行自蒸馏来提供特权监督。在nuScenes数据集上，该方法将G=4平均L2误差从1.848降至1.500米，并将持久失败率从13.03%降至11.20%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GRPO等相对策略优化方法只在每个rollout组内比较奖励差异，当所有采样轨迹都很差时，这种相对信号虽能对失败进行排序，但无法识别失败区域之外的行为，导致策略无法有效逃离失败区域。现有方法缺乏将未解决的失败转化为有效监督信号的机制。

Method: FIRE-VLA包含以下关键设计：（1）检测低奖励、低多样性的轨迹组，将其判定为"未解决失败"；（2）从冻结的回合起始模型副本进行自蒸馏，教师和学生参数规模相同，但教师能观察隐藏的未来轨迹；（3）监督信号跟随学生生成的前缀，且仅限制在答案token上；（4）GRPO仍然对每个轨迹组保持激活；（5）更新后的策略作为下一轮教师，使失败分布随策略演化而动态变化，无需更大的外部教师。

Result: 在6,019个来自150个保留nuScenes场景的样本上，FIRE-VLA保持了可比的单样本规划能力，将G=4平均L2从1.848降至1.500米，评估持久失败率从13.03%降至11.20%。平均误差的降低主要来源于罕见的严重rollout改进，而非普通轨迹上的均匀提升。

Conclusion: FIRE-VLA通过将"未解决的失败"转化为可用的特权监督信号，在不引入更大外部教师模型的情况下实现了自进化的策略改进。该方法的主要收益集中在消除罕见但严重的失败rollout上，为自动驾驶VLA模型的强化学习提供了一种新的失败感知训练范式。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning improves autonomous-driving vision-language-action (VLA) models by evaluating trajectories sampled from the current policy. Group relative policy optimization (GRPO) learns from reward differences within each rollout group. When all sampled trajectories are poor, this relative signal can rank failures without identifying behavior outside the failed region. We introduce FIRE-VLA, a failure-informed self-evolution framework that converts such unresolved failures into privileged supervision for the next policy. Low-reward, low-diversity groups trigger self-distillation from a frozen round-start copy of the same model. Teacher and student have the same parameter scale, but only the teacher observes the hidden future trajectory. Supervision follows the student's generated prefix and is restricted to answer tokens, while GRPO remains active for every group. The updated policy supplies the teacher for the next round, allowing the routed failure distribution to change with the policy without requiring a larger external teacher. Starting from the same Qwen2.5-VL-3B SFT checkpoint, the comparison matches student rollout and policy-update counts. On 6,019 examples from 150 held-out nuScenes scenes, FIRE-VLA retains comparable single-sample planning, reduces G=4 mean L2 from 1.848 to 1.500 m, and lowers evaluation-persistent failure prevalence from 13.03% to 11.20%. The reduction in mean error arises mainly from rare severe rollouts rather than uniform improvement across ordinary trajectories.

</details>


### [111] [Capstan-driven Continuum Surgical Robot: Design, Modeling, and Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13396)
*Gang Zhang,Yufu Qiu,Junyan Yan,Wenhui Zeng,Wenlong Lu,Shing Shin Cheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于"驱动-感知协同设计"理念的集成设计-建模-传感框架，通过在马达安装支架中引入柔性元件实现实时缆绳张力测量，并结合多体短厚梁模型的并行计算框架以及近端多维力/力矩传感器的测量锚定，实现了对缆绳驱动连续体手术机器人（单段和双段构型）的实时尖端位姿估计以及接触力与位置感知。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 紧凑型缆绳驱动的连续体手术机器人长期面临形状和力传感的瓶颈，主要难点在于在狭小的绞盘组件内获取缆绳张力信息十分困难。

Method: 提出"驱动-感知协同设计"的集成设计-建模-传感方法：1）在马达安装支架中引入柔性元件，利用缆绳反作用力产生的微变形实现实时张力测量；2）基于多体短厚梁模型提出并行计算框架，捕获短梁段的剪切效应和多缆绳协同交互，实现实时性能；3）结合近端多维力/力矩传感器作为附加测量锚点，实现稳定的形状和力传感。

Result: 在单段和双段构型的连续体机器人上进行了实验验证，结果表明该框架能够实现实时尖端位姿估计以及接触力与位置感知，同时不牺牲紧凑的绞盘架构。

Conclusion: 该框架在不破坏紧凑绞盘架构的前提下实现了缆绳张力反馈，使缆绳驱动的连续体手术机器人具备集成的感知能力，为紧凑型连续体手术机器人的形状与力传感提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Shape and force sensing have long been critical bottlenecks in the development of compact capstan-driven continuum surgical robots, primarily due to the difficulty of obtaining cable tension information within the confined capstan assembly. To overcome these challenges, this paper presents an integrated design-modeling-sensing approach based on the concept of actuation-perception co-design. A compliant element is introduced into the motor mounting bracket of the drive system, enabling micro-deformation under the cable reaction force and thereby allowing real-time cable tension measurement without occupying the compact capstan space. To address the modeling complexity arising from unconventional joint configurations introduced by the spatial cable routing strategy, a parallel computation framework based on a multibody short-thick-beam model is proposed, which captures shear effects in short beam segments and synergistic multi-cable interactions while achieving real-time performance. Building on this framework, stable shape and force sensing is achieved by incorporating a proximal multi-axis force/torque sensor as an additional measurement anchor. Following this design-modeling-sensing framework, capstan-driven continuum surgical robots with single- and dual-segment configurations are developed. Experimental results validate the proposed framework in both single- and dual-segment continuum robots, demonstrating real-time tip pose estimation together with contact force and location perception. By enabling cable tension feedback without compromising the compact capstan architecture, the proposed framework makes integrated perception feasible for capstan-driven continuum surgical robots.

</details>


### [112] [Deliberate Practice: Learning Robot Skills under a Budget](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13415)
*Shivam Vats,Sudarshan Harithas,Mete Tuluhan Akbulut,Arvind Raghunathan,George Konidaris*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种名为"刻意练习"(Deliberate Practice, DP)的主动技能学习算法，用于在有限的练习预算下自主学习机器人技能。该算法通过双线性规划精确计算预算最优的技能分配方案，在模拟和真实世界的长时程操作任务中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在顺序任务中，机器人需要在有限的练习预算内自主选择学习哪些技能，以最大化累积奖励。现有方法缺乏对预算分配的理论保证，而计算最优分配需要处理组合规模的技能规划问题，极具挑战性。

Method: 提出Deliberate Practice算法，通过估计技能掌握所需时间和技能解锁任务计划的累积奖励来构建预算最优分配问题。核心贡献是将该组合优化问题转化为一个双线性规划(bilinear program)，可利用现成的求解器精确求解。

Result: 在模拟和真实世界的长时程操作任务中，该方法能让机器人利用有限的练习时间获取有用的策略，并改进长时程规划表现，证明了预算最优分配的有效性。

Conclusion: 该方法为机器人在有限预算下的技能学习提供了可验证的理论保证，通过双线性规划实现了预算最优的主动学习，在实际机器人任务中验证了其可行性。

Abstract: We consider the problem of autonomously learning robot skills under a limited practice budget for sequential tasks. We propose an active skill learning algorithm, \emph{Deliberate Practice (DP)}, that computes a provably \emph{budget-optimal} allocation---practicing skills that maximize expected cumulative reward while being learnable within the budget. DP estimates both the time needed to master skills and the cumulative reward of the task plans that the skills unlock. Computing a budget-optimal allocation is challenging as it requires reasoning about combinatorially many skill plans over a large practice budget. Our key contribution is a bilinear program that can compute this exactly using off-the-shelf solvers. Through simulated and real-world experiments on long-horizon manipulation tasks, we show that our approach allows robots to optimally use limited practice time to acquire useful policies and improve long-horizon planning.

</details>


### [113] [Attention from Action, for Action: Emergent Visual Bottlenecks for Policy Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13422)
*Zheyu Zhuang,Ruiyu Wang,Nick Heppert,Johannes Fabian Hahn,Abhinav Valada,Florian T. Pokorny,Danica Kragic*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Seeker，一种任务和状态条件化的视觉读取机制，从动作监督中学习注意力，生成随任务进度演化的兴趣区域（ROI），无需外部空间标签，显著提升机器人视觉运动学习的数据效率和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的ROI接口依赖外部空间标签（如注视、物体类别或可供性标注），而无标签的替代方案（如从轨迹中检测夹爪或运动事件）编码了固定的时间选择、代理点和裁剪尺度，当控制所需的视觉证据远离末端执行器或随任务进度变化时，固定裁剪会变得错位，影响学习效果。

Method: Seeker从冻结的DINOv3特征出发，迭代地更新查询以收集视觉证据，仅通过动作监督产生随任务进度感知的ROI。该学习得到的ROI作为空间接口，用于RGB裁剪、掩码引导的背景增强和点云过滤。

Result: 在仿真和真实环境中，Seeker相较无裁剪、增强和动作派生裁剪基线均提升了数据效率和鲁棒性。在真实机器人上，Seeker将领域内成功率的平均最佳基线从48.3%提升到76.7%，在光照/背景变化下的成功率从20.0%提升到60.0%。

Conclusion: Seeker证明了从动作监督中学习注意力是可行的，所学的进展感知ROI能够有效分离"看哪里"与"如何行动"，在不依赖外部标签的情况下显著改善视觉运动策略的性能和泛化能力。

Abstract: Visual bottlenecks that focus policy inputs on regions of interest (ROIs) can improve data-efficient visuomotor learning by separating where to look from how to act. Many ROI interfaces rely on external spatial labels, such as gaze, object classes, or affordance annotations. Label-free alternatives often derive crops from trajectories by detecting gripper or motion events and centering a fixed crop at the projected end-effector. Such action-derived crops are useful spatial priors that require no additional labels, but they encode fixed choices about event timing, proxy points, and crop scale. When the visual evidence needed for control lies away from the end-effector or changes continuously with task progress, these crops can become misaligned. We propose Seeker, a task- and state-conditioned readout that learns attention from action. Starting from frozen DINOv3 features, Seeker iteratively updates a query with gathered visual evidence, producing progression-aware ROIs solely from action supervision. The learned ROI serves as a spatial interface for RGB cropping, mask-guided background augmentation, and point-cloud filtering. In simulation and the real world, Seeker improves data efficiency and robustness over no-crop, augmentation, and action-derived crop baselines. On real robots, Seeker raises average in-domain success from the best baseline's 48.3% to 76.7% and success under lighting/background shifts from 20.0% to 60.0%.

</details>


### [114] [ContactGuard: Pre-Contact Execution Monitoring with Action-Conditioned Latent World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13438)
*Gehan Zheng,Matthew Johnson-Roberson,Weiming Zhi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出ContactGuard，一种接触前执行监控器，用于分块视觉运动策略。它在潜在视觉空间中预测策略计划动作的短期后果，并在预测到潜在失败时中止执行。其潜在世界模型从无标注机器人轨迹中训练，通过一个小型标注接触前片段集训练轻量级失败探针，在实时部署中作为接触前中止信号，无需修改底层策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 接触丰富的机器人操作中，失败往往在机器人已经接触物体之后才被检测到。在腕部相机设置中，虽然近距离的夹爪-物体视图有助于观察接触，但不良的接近轨迹可能在传统检测器反应之前就已经推挤、错过、滑移或扰动物体，因此需要在接触发生前进行失败预测。

Method: ContactGuard包含两个核心组件：(1) 潜在世界模型——从无标注机器人轨迹中训练，以预测在计划动作下紧凑的多视图视觉嵌入，避免像素级视频预测；(2) 轻量级失败探针——从少量标注的接触前片段中训练。部署时，在即将接触的事件前锚定预测，在策略自身动作下滚动模型前向，并验证预测的接触后潜在状态。

Result: 在真实世界的接触丰富操作任务中，ContactGuard比直接预测和损坏动作消融基线更准确地预测失败，并能作为接触前中止信号迁移到实时机器人上，且无需修改底层策略。

Conclusion: ContactGuard通过潜在空间预测实现了可靠的接触前失败检测，能在不干扰原始策略的情况下提供提前中止能力，有效弥补了传统失败检测机制在接触前阶段的盲区。

Abstract: Contact-rich manipulation failures are often detected only after the robot has committed to contact. This is especially limiting in wrist-camera setups: close gripper--object views help observe contact, but a poor approach may already push, miss, slip, or disturb the object before conventional detectors react. We introduce \emph{ContactGuard}, a pre-contact execution monitor for chunked visuomotor policies. Given the policy's planned action chunk, ContactGuard predicts its short-horizon consequence in latent visual space and aborts if the predicted future latent indicates likely failure. Its latent world model is trained from unlabelled robot trajectories to predict compact multi-view visual embeddings under planned actions, avoiding pixel-level video prediction. A lightweight failure probe is then trained from a small labelled set of pre-contact clips. At deployment, ContactGuard anchors prediction before an imminent contact event, rolls the model forward under the policy's own actions, and verifies the predicted post-contact latent. Across real-world contact-rich manipulation tasks, ContactGuard predicts failure more accurately than direct and corrupted-action ablations, and transfers to live robot as a pre-contact abort signal without modifying the underlying policy.

</details>


### [115] [Mind the Context: Continual Learning of Socially Appropriate Robot Actions via Environmental-Social Disentanglement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13448)
*Rafal Robert Karpinski,Fethiye Irmak Dogan,Nikhil Churamani,Yiming Luo,Maartje M. A. de Graaf,Davide Dell'Anna,Hatice Gunes*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出显式解耦双分支（EDD）框架，用于社交机器人在多样化室内环境中的领域增量持续学习，通过显式分离环境与社会智能体知识并结合重放机制，在多个室内领域间学习适当的机器人动作，超越了多种最先进基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交机器人需要在不同环境中表现适当的社交行为，但环境规范无法事先全部预知，需要持续学习能力。现有工作未解决领域增量设置，即在逐渐遇到多样化场景时，环境线索（如空间布局）与社会线索（如人员位置）共同决定机器人动作的适当性。

Method: 提出EDD框架，采用双分支结构显式解耦环境相关知识与社交智能体相关知识，利用基于重放的排练机制缓解灾难性遗忘，在多个室内领域（起居室、会议室、办公室、走廊等）中学习清洁、服务、开启对话等机器人动作的适当性。

Result: 实验表明EDD在多个最先进基线上表现更优；消融研究进一步评估了不同解耦策略的效果以及对领域顺序的敏感性。

Conclusion: EDD框架有效解决了社交机器人领域增量持续学习问题，显式解耦环境和社交知识有助于在多样化室内环境中学习并保持适当的机器人动作知识。

Abstract: Social robots are expected to operate across diverse environments, where similar arrangements can imply different socially appropriate actions, e.g., starting a conversation may be acceptable in a crowded home but disruptive in an office meeting. Because such norms and environments cannot all be anticipated in advance, robots require continual learning (CL) to adapt from sequential experience while retaining previously acquired knowledge. Prior work has studied CL for generating socially appropriate robot actions, but it has not addressed domain-incremental settings in which the robot incrementally encounters diverse contexts (e.g., living room, meeting room, office, hallway), where both environmental (e.g., whether the space is open or cluttered with furniture) and social cues (e.g., how people or other agents are positioned around the robot) jointly shape the appropriateness of robot actions. We address this gap with the Explicit Disentanglement Dual-Branch (EDD) framework. EDD explicitly separates environmental and social-agent related knowledge and uses replay-based rehearsal to mitigate forgetting while learning the appropriateness of robot actions (e.g., cleaning, serving, starting a conversation) across several indoor domains. Experiments show that EDD outperforms several state-of-the-art baselines, and ablation studies further evaluate different disentanglement strategies and the sensitivity to domain ordering. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/Cambridge-AFAR/Mind-the-Context.git.

</details>


### [116] [Decoding Task Progress from VLA Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13474)
*Atiksh Bhardwaj,Edward Weiyi Duan,Prithwish Dan,Wei-Chiu Ma,Preston Culbertson*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究视觉-语言-动作模型（VLA）的内部表征，发现任务进度（轨迹中剩余时间的归一化值）可以从π0.5模型的残差流中线性读取，该信号在预训练阶段就已存在，可作为无标签的OOD检测器用于监控部署中的策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型正快速走向实际部署，但缺乏理解其内部表征和运行时监控的基本工具，需要可解释的工具来监控这些通用操作策略。

Method: 利用机械可解释性的思想，对π0.5模型的残差流进行线性探针（linear probe）分析，检测任务进度信号的线性可读性；同时评估该探针在未见任务上的泛化能力、语言反事实下的变化，以及在多提示数据训练下的表现。

Result: 任务进度信号在预训练的PaliGemma骨干中就已存在（无需机器人数据训练）；单个线性探针可以泛化到未见任务，在多提示数据训练下会随语言反事实变化，但无法有效引导策略；该探针作为无标签OOD检测器可检测停滞的任务进度，性能与最先进方法相当。

Conclusion: VLA模型包含丰富、线性可读的语义内部表征（如任务进度），学习读取这些信号为监控部署中的视觉运动策略提供了一条轻量级、可解释的路径。

Abstract: Vision-language-action models (VLAs) are moving rapidly towards deployment as general-purpose manipulation policies, but we currently lack basic tools for understanding what these models represent internally or for monitoring them at runtime. Leveraging ideas from mechanistic interpretability, we probe the residual stream of $π_{0.5}$ and find that task progress, the normalized time remaining in a trajectory, is linearly readable from the activations. We find that this signal is present in the pretrained PaliGemma backbone prior to training on any robot-specific data. A single linear probe generalizes to unseen tasks and varies under language counterfactuals when trained on multi-prompt data, but does not enable meaningful steering of the policy. These properties make the signal directly useful for instrumenting deployed VLAs. We use the probe as a simple label-free OOD detector, which detects stalled task progress, and find it competitive with state-of-the-art methods. Our results suggest that VLAs have rich, linearly readable internal representations of semantic quantities like task progress, and that learning to read these signals offers a lightweight, interpretable path toward monitoring deployed visuomotor policies.

</details>


### [117] [A Browser-Native Digital Test Range for Benchmarking 4D Ocean-Glider Planning Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13511)
*Edward Holmberg,Elias Ioup,Mahdi Abdelguerfi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于浏览器的海洋滑翔机规划器数字试验场（Digital Test Range），通过统一的"规划-观测"契约，使手动路径、内置算法和外部规划器输出能在可复现的四维环境中进行公平评估。实验验证了54个任务全部成功完成，并通过OSSE实验揭示了规划器排名与科学任务之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 海洋滑翔机规划器的反复实地评估面临诸多限制：车辆稀缺、操作人员与资源部署成本高，且海洋条件无法重置以公平比较竞争算法。现有评估手段缺乏可复现性，亟需一种无需安装、可在浏览器中运行的数字试验环境。

Method: 构建一个引导式、免安装的浏览器原生数字试验场，将选定区域转化为可复现的四维实验。系统覆盖从区域选择、任务范围测深、时间/深度强迫、科学目标设定、可选的子任务分解、路径规划、流场平流执行、观测生成到评分的完整流程。核心贡献是一个统一的规划-观测契约，整合车辆、传感和评估者假设；同时通过Pyodide/WebAssembly实现GliderFlight 1.2.0的浏览器原生高保真模拟。

Result: 在受控OSSE实验中，5个经典规划器在2个场景、3个确定性种子和60小时标定时间范围内完成全部54个任务且无硬性违规；规划器排名与潜深策略效应揭示了操作-科学权衡。公开部署提供了现场参照审计；GliderFlight 1.2.0实现了原生到浏览器的功能对等。

Conclusion: 该数字试验场提供了科学可追溯、组件合格的操作空间，适用于任务规模的部署前实验，为海洋滑翔机规划器的公平评估提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Repeated in-situ evaluation of ocean-glider planners requires scarce vehicles, operators, deployment and recovery resources, and ocean conditions that cannot be reset for competing algorithms. We present a guided, installation-free browser-native digital test range that transforms a selected region into a reproducible four-dimensional experiment. The system leads users from regional domain selection through mission-scoped bathymetry, time/depth forcing, science objectives, optional task decomposition, route specification, current-advected execution, observation generation, and scoring. Its primary contribution is a common plan-to-observation contract unifying vehicle, sensing, and evaluator assumptions across manual routes, transparent built-in algorithms, and imported classical or learned-planner outputs, while exported artifacts form dataset-ready records. A controlled Observing System Simulation Experiment (OSSE) evaluates five classical planners in two episodes, three deterministic seeds, and a calibrated 60-hour horizon. All 54 missions completed and recovered without hard violations, while planner rankings and dive-policy effects revealed operational-scientific tradeoffs. An authentic public deployment supplied a field-referenced audit to scope current kinematic boundaries. Separately, source-locked GliderFlight 1.2.0 achieved native-to-browser parity through Pyodide/WebAssembly, establishing a pathway for high-fidelity multi-tier simulation. The resulting operational space is scientifically traceable and component-qualified for mission-scale pre-deployment experimentation.

</details>


### [118] [HumanTracker: Towards Comprehensive and Human-Aligned Motion Tracking Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13555)
*Dairu Liu,Zekun Qi,Jiayu Zeng,Ruixi Yu,Yu Guan,Yintianrun Zhang,Xuchuan Chen,Sikai Liang,Zekai Li,Chenghuai Lin,Xinqiang Yu,Wenyao Zhang,He Wang,Li Yi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出HumanTracker基准和HumanScore指标，用于使类人运动跟踪评估与人类感知对齐，覆盖153小时专业动作数据和12K运动对训练，能更好地预测人类偏好并揭示接触和稳定性故障。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的类人运动跟踪评估与人类视觉感知不一致——运动学误差只计算逐帧位姿差异，却忽略了人们最关心的物理伪影（如脚步打滑、触地时机错误、支撑不稳定等）。同时现有测试套件规模小、多样性不足，难以覆盖接触密集和长时程行为。

Method: 1) 构建HumanTracker基准：包含约153小时多位专业表演者的光学运动轨迹，组织为四类运动家族，并配有文本标签用于细粒度诊断；2) 提出HumanScore：一种偏好对齐的评估指标，在12K运动对（含24K条运动）上训练。

Result: 在多个代表性最新跟踪器上，HumanScore比传统运动学指标更好地预测人类偏好，并能够揭示运动学指标经常遗漏的接触和稳定性失败。

Conclusion: HumanTracker和HumanScore使类人运动跟踪评估既与人类感知对齐又具有可扩展性，能更准确地反映实际跟踪质量。

Abstract: Humanoid motion tracking is central to teleoperation and whole-body imitation, yet evaluation often disagrees with what people perceive in videos. Kinematic errors average per-frame pose differences but miss the physical artifacts that matter most, particularly unstable support and incorrect contacts such as foot skating and mistimed touch-downs. Meanwhile, widely used test suites are small and lack the diversity needed to stress contact-rich, long-horizon behaviors. We introduce HumanTracker to make humanoid tracking evaluation both perceptually aligned and scalable. The HumanTracker benchmark contains approximately 153 hours of optical motion trajectories from multiple professional performers, organized into four motion families with text labels for fine-grained diagnosis. We further propose HumanScore, a preference-aligned metric trained on 12K motion pairs containing 24K motions. Across representative state-of-the-art trackers, HumanScore better predicts human preferences and reveals contact and stability failures that kinematic metrics often miss.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [119] [LoKiFormer: Locality-aware Attention with Decoupled Knowledge Memory for Efficient Large Language Model Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12419)
*Qiuwu Chen,Zimo Liu,Yuchen Li,Ying Sun,Yifan Zhang,Zhijie Qiu,Zeng You,Ryan Dong,Simeng Ma,Yaofo Chen,Mingkui Tan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LoKiFormer，一种新型大语言模型架构，通过局部融合注意力（LFA）和知识记忆模块（KMM）分别解决自注意力缺乏局部归纳偏置以及MoE隐式耦合知识存储与计算路径的问题，预训练收敛速度比基线模型快1.33倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM架构在预训练中存在两个低效问题：1）自注意力缺乏对局部信息的显式归纳偏置，导致序列内部局部信息被冗余建模；2）MoE将知识存储与计算路径隐式耦合，阻碍了对序列外部全局知识的灵活访问。

Method: 在标准解码器上增加两个模块：1）局部融合注意力（LFA），将卷积融合引入注意力机制，显式捕获局部模式并使注意力作用于更具信息量的表征；2）知识记忆模块（KMM），引入可参数化的键值记忆，将全局知识显式存储在可寻址的槽位中，实现存储与计算解耦并支持直接知识检索。

Result: 实验结果表明，LoKiFormer在预训练中的收敛速度比基线模型快1.33倍。

Conclusion: LoKiFormer通过局部与全局两个层面的显式信息整合机制，在预训练效率上优于现有LLM架构。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have achieved remarkable breakthroughs across various applications. However, their architectures remain inefficient in pretraining due to two main limitations: (i) self-attention lacks an explicit inductive bias for locality, leading to redundant modeling of sequence-internal local information; (ii) mixture-of-experts (MoE) implicitly couples knowledge storage with computational pathways, hindering flexible access to sequence-external global knowledge. To overcome these limitations, we propose LoKiFormer, a novel LLM architecture that augments the standard decoder with two dedicated modules: 1) Local Fusion Attention (LFA), which incorporates a convolutional fusion to attention, explicitly capturing local patterns and allowing the attention to operate on more informative representations; 2) Knowledge Memory Module (KMM), which introduces a parametric key-value memory that explicitly stores global knowledge in addressable slots, decoupling storage from computation and enabling direct knowledge retrieval. Together, these modules enable LoKiFormer to achieve more efficient and effective integration of information at both levels. Experimental results show that LoKiFormer converges 1.33x faster in pre-training than baseline models, underscoring its superiority over existing LLM architectures.

</details>


### [120] [Which Site, and When: A Free-Satellite-Data Test of Himalayan Glacial Lake Bursts, Landslides, and Ice Floods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12422)
*Matthew Kahn,Milan Arjel,Nirmala Adhikari,Mingmar Sherpa,James Pope*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文利用免费卫星数据（雷达干涉测量和卫星气象）构建了尼泊尔喜马拉雅地区冰湖溃决、滑坡和冰川小洪水三种灾害的预测模型，发现气象时序可有效判定触发时机（ROC 0.73–0.83），而地形对易发性排序的效果有限（真实评分0.54–0.76），深度学习模型未能显著超越简单梯度提升基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前期可行性研究已证明雷达干涉可识别冰湖坝体变形、气象数据可判定湖体承压时段，但缺乏将两者整合的预测模型。本文旨在填补这一空白，提出并评估能够预测"哪个地点易发"以及"何时触发"的模型。

Method: 基于589条HMAGLOFDB冰湖溃决记录和数千条滑坡目录，将每个事件与相似但未失稳的地点匹配，并在空间交叉验证（按地图瓦片整体留出）下评测模型。采用梯度提升基线、五个深度学习模型及三规则决策树，对每种灾害分别回答"易发性"与"触发时机"两个问题。

Result: 气象前置条件在触发时机判定上表现良好（大溃决ROC 0.73、滑坡0.83、小洪水0.82）；地形排名的真实表现大幅低于朴素评分（分别为0.76、0.71、0.54），后者虚高源于灾害在湿润区聚集。大溃决信号在尼泊尔单区域可达0.89。深度学习模型未能决定性超越梯度提升基线，湖泊类灾害中简单基线反而胜出。

Conclusion: 免费卫星数据足以支撑灾害易发性和触发时机的部分预测能力，但地形信号被区域降水偏好严重干扰，需谨慎解读。作者提供了一份尼泊尔优先级观测清单作为辅助工具（非预测），并指出了免费数据的应用边界。

Abstract: Two free satellite signals carry real information about glacial-lake outburst risk in the Nepal Himalaya: radar interferometry sees a moraine dam slowly sagging, and satellite weather marks the weeks when a primed lake is under stress. A companion feasibility study found that deformation indicates which lake is destabilizing and weather indicates when it is at risk, but proposed no predictive model. To address this gap, we propose and evaluate models that predict which site is susceptible and when a trigger arrives. We test three related hazards on free data alone: large moraine- and ice-dammed bursts, rainfall-triggered landslides, and smaller floods from ponds on and around a glacier. Each hazard gets two questions, never blended. Using 589 dated outbursts from HMAGLOFDB and several thousand catalogued landslides, we match each event against similar but unfailed sites, and hold every model to a strong simple baseline under spatial cross-validation that withholds whole map tiles, so no model succeeds by recognising a trained-on neighbourhood. Antecedent weather times the trigger at ROC 0.73 for big bursts, 0.83 for landslides, and 0.82 for small floods. Terrain ranks susceptibility only in part: scored naively it appears near 0.9, largely because catalogued failures cluster in wetter ranges; matched against comparable nearby sites the honest figures are 0.76, 0.71, and 0.54 (no better than chance). The burst signal holds within single regions, reaching 0.89 in Nepal alone. Five deep-learning models do not decisively beat a simple gradient-boosted baseline. Three score marginally higher on landslides, a hint too small to confirm. For the lake hazards the baseline wins outright, reproduced by a three-rule decision tree on ruggedness and monsoon rainfall. We close with a ranked Nepal watchlist, a prioritisation aid, not a prediction, and note where free data reaches its limits.

</details>


### [121] [MARCH: Scaling Recurrent Memory with Content-Routed State Anchors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12435)
*Ming Zhang,Kaisen Yang,Shu Yu,Ermo Hua,Ning Ding,Xia Hu,Bowen Zhou,Chaochao Lu,Youbang Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MARCH是一种新型网络架构，通过周期性缓存循环状态检查点作为"状态锚点"，并利用内容条件的锚点键进行注意力聚合，在保持长序列计算效率的同时超越固定大小状态空间的限制，在常识推理、LongBench和上下文检索任务上优于多种线性注意力变体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer虽然具有强大的长上下文检索能力，但训练时面临二次方计算复杂度、推理时键值缓存线性增长的问题。循环模型虽然解码高效、将历史压缩为固定大小状态，但在召回密集型任务上表现不佳，因为早期关联常被后续更新覆盖，只保留最近上下文信息。因此需要一个既能保持循环模型计算效率、又能突破固定大小状态限制的架构。

Method: MARCH周期性将累积的循环状态检查点缓存为"状态锚点"，并为每个锚点关联一个紧凑的、内容条件的锚点键。它维护一个可随上下文长度增长的记忆库，在历史分辨率和记忆成本之间提供可控权衡。在每个token处，MARCH生成锚点查询，对所有因果可用的状态锚点进行注意力操作，输出为对所有历史锚点沿当前状态进行注意力式聚合的结果。

Result: 经过标准预训练后，MARCH在常识推理、LongBench和上下文检索任务上持续优于多种线性注意力变体，证明内容路由的状态缓存能显著增强循环长程记忆，同时保持其原生计算路径。

Conclusion: 内容路由的状态缓存（MARCH）能有效结合Transformer的灵活长程记忆与循环模型的高效解码优势，为长序列建模提供了一种兼具性能和效率的解决方案。

Abstract: Transformers owe much of their strong long-context retrieval capability to a token-level memory that grows with context length. This flexibility, however, incurs a quadratic computation complexity during training and a key--value cache that grows linearly during autoregressive inference. Recurrent alternatives offer efficient decoding by compressing the entire history into a fixed-size state, but often underperform on recall-intensive tasks since earlier associations usually get overwritten by subsequent updates, and only the most recent contextual information is retained. In this paper, we introduce Memory-Anchor Routing across Context History (MARCH), a network architecture that effectively scales state-space models beyond a fixed-size dimension, while maintaining computational efficiency over long-sequences. MARCH periodically caches cumulative recurrent-state checkpoints as state anchors and associates each anchor with a compact, content-conditioned anchor key. This lets MARCH maintain a memory bank, which can grow as context length increases, providing a controllable trade-off between historical resolution and memory cost. At each token, MARCH produces an anchor query to attend all causally available state anchors, and the output is calculated as an attention-style aggregation over all historical anchors along the current state. We show that after standard pretraining, MARCH consistently outperforms multiple linear attention variants across commonsense reasoning, LongBench, and in-context retrieval. These results demonstrate that content-routed state caching substantially strengthens recurrent long-range memory while preserving its native computation path.

</details>


### [122] [Multi-AUV Ad-hoc network-based Target Tracking: A Value Gradient Guidance Multi-Agent Diffusion Reinforcement Learning Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12436)
*Jiaao Ma,Chuan Lin,Guangjie Han,Shengchao Zhu,Qian Zhu,Ying Liu,Zhenyu Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出VGG-MADiffRL算法和MDCA三层控制架构，用于多AUV自组网协同目标跟踪，通过值梯度引导扩散策略的去噪过程，并采用孪生值网络联合优化，解决了现有MARL方法在高维状态动作建模和噪声敏感策略生成方面的不稳定问题，实验验证了更快的收敛速度和更高的跟踪精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多AUV自组网目标跟踪面临声学通信受限、动态拓扑和海洋扰动等挑战，现有基于MARL的方法存在高维联合状态动作建模困难、策略生成对噪声敏感等问题，导致训练不稳定、跟踪性能退化，需要新的方法解决这些问题。

Method: 提出VGG-MADiffRL（值梯度引导的多智能体扩散强化学习算法）和MDCA（基于扩散的分层控制架构）。MDCA采用三层闭环控制框架：全局智能控制层、本地在线训练层和物理动作执行层。VGG-MADiffRL基于扩散策略，在逆向去噪过程中引入值梯度引导动作生成，并采用孪生值网络联合优化与软目标更新以缓解过估计和训练振荡。同时，建模了声呐探测机制和洋流扰动，将协同跟踪问题形式化为MDP。

Result: 实验结果表明，VGG-MADiffRL在协同跟踪场景中持续实现更快的收敛速度、更高的跟踪精度和更平滑的训练动态，验证了其有效性和在动态水下环境中的实际工程价值。

Conclusion: 基于扩散策略并引入值梯度引导的多智能体强化学习方案有效解决了多AUV协同跟踪中的训练不稳定问题，三层控制架构MDCA实现了任务分配、局部决策和执行反馈的协同优化，为动态水下环境中的多AUV协同目标跟踪提供了可行且有效的技术方案。

Abstract: Multi-AUV ad-hoc network-based target tracking requires networked autonomous underwater vehicles (AUVs) to cooperatively track maneuvering targets under constrained acoustic communication, dynamic topology, and uncertain ocean disturbances. Although multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) enables decentralized coordination through centralized training, existing methods suffer from high-dimensional joint state-action modeling, noise-sensitive policy generation, leading to unstable training and degraded tracking. To address these issues, we propose VGG-MADiffRL, a value-gradient-guided multi-agent diffusion RL algorithm, and MDCA, a diffusion?based hierarchical control architecture. Leveraging underwater mission characteristics, we model sonar detection mechanisms and ocean current disturbances, formulating cooperative tracking for multi-AUV ad-hoc networks as an MDP. The proposed MDCA constitutes a three-tier closed-loop control framework: a global intelligent control layer, a local online training layer, and a physical action execution layer. This structure enables synergistic optimization across task allocation, local decision processes, and execution feedback. Within MDCA, the local online training layer is the policy learning framework; VGG-MADiffRL builds on diffusion policies and incorporates value gradients to guide action generation in the reverse denoising process, steering the generated actions towards higher expected returns. It employs twin value networks with joint optimization and soft target updates to mitigate overestimation and training oscillations, promoting more stable convergence. Experimental results show that VGG-MADiffRL consistently achieves faster convergence, higher tracking accuracy, and smoother training dynamics in cooperative tracking scenarios, validating its effectiveness and practical engineering value in dynamic underwater settings.

</details>


### [123] [Unifying Generative Models with Path Integrals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12438)
*Ramon Winterhalder*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个统一的生成建模框架，将基于流、扩散、变分和对抗模型统一为一个单一的主行动路径积分，并利用MSRJD形式进行微扰展开。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的生成模型（流模型、扩散模型、变分模型、对抗模型）各自独立发展，缺乏统一的理论框架，难以相互借鉴和进行系统性的理论分析。

Method: 将生成建模表述为路径积分，利用Martin-Siggia-Rose-Janssen-de Dominicis（MSRJD）形式分离自由与相互作用概率流，并引入费曼图微扰理论进行展开。

Result: 扩张产生了一阶环修正，可在无需随机采样成本的情况下改进确定性采样器；在可解和非线性漂移上，将53%的树级误差降至1.6%。不完美的学习分数以插入项形式出现，产生了响应加权的分数匹配目标。

Conclusion: 该框架为生成模型提供了统一的场论视角，开辟了基于图微扰理论、对称性驱动设计及EFT功率计数的新研究方向。

Abstract: We formulate generative modeling as a path integral in which flow-based, diffusion-based, variational, and adversarial models arise as different evaluation principles for a single master action. Its Martin-Siggia-Rose-Janssen-de~Dominicis (MSRJD) form separates free from interacting probability flows and opens them to diagrammatic perturbation theory. The expansion yields a one-loop correction to deterministic samplers at no stochastic-sampling cost, which we validate on solvable and nonlinear drifts, where it reduces a 53 % tree-level error to 1.6 %. Imperfect learned scores enter as insertions and yield a response-weighted score-matching objective, and symmetry-equivariant drift design becomes an operator expansion with EFT power counting.

</details>


### [124] [Dual Spatial-Temporal Attribution: Architecture-Aligned Post-Hoc Explainability for Recurrent Graph Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12441)
*Iyad Assaad Nekka,Hamida Seba,Khaled Walid Hidouci,Karima Amrouche*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了X-AddGraph框架，为动态图异常检测模型AddGraph提供严格的事后（post-hoc）可解释性。该框架基于双时空归因（DSTA）机制，包含三个组件（梯度归因用于空间维、注意力权重用于短期时间维、梯度回滚用于长期时间维），在不改变检测性能的前提下（Delta AUC = 0）为边级异常检测提供可审计的解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态图异常检测模型（如AddGraph）虽准确率高但不可解释，在需要审计和信任的受监管信息系统中，仅输出异常分数而无法说明理由是不可接受的。AddGraph作为基础性的GCN+GRU框架此前从未配备任何形式的可解释性，这一空白亟待填补。

Method: 提出X-AddGraph框架，构建在双时空归因（DSTA）机制上，其三个组件分别对应AddGraph的三个架构模块：(1) 基于梯度的当前邻接结构空间相关性归因；(2) 直接读取推理过程中已计算好的上下文注意力权重（短期时间维，零额外成本）；(3) 通过循环隐藏状态进行梯度回滚（长期时间维）。检测器保持冻结状态。

Result: 在UCI Message基准上，训练的AddGraph基线达到平均每快照AUC 0.8705，超过原论文结果；X-AddGraph复现每个分数并添加解释，检测性能完全保持（Delta AUC=0，精确到小数点后十位）。在四类边群体（高置信度真阳性、低置信度真阳性、假阳性、随机样本）评估中，长期归因识别历史快照的反事实信号显著优于随机选择（0.127 vs 0.074）。

Conclusion: X-AddGraph证明了在不牺牲检测性能的前提下，可以为动态图异常检测模型提供完整、可审计的事后解释能力，尤其长期时间归因能提供空间盲解释器无法实现的历史反事实信号识别能力。代码已开源以支持完全可复现性。

Abstract: Deep learning detectors for anomalies in dynamic graphs have reached strong accuracy, yet they remain opaque: when an edge is flagged, the analyst receives a score but no reason. This opacity is untenable in the cooperative, regulated information systems where such detectors are deployed, where automated decisions must be auditable and trustworthy. We address this gap for AddGraph, the foundational GCN+GRU framework for edge-level anomaly detection in dynamic graphs, which to our knowledge has never been equipped with any form of explainability. We present a strictly post-hoc explainability framework, X-AddGraph, built on a Dual Spatial-Temporal Attribution (DSTA) mechanism whose three components are each aligned with one of AddGraph's architectural modules: a gradient-based relevance attribution over the current adjacency structure (spatial), a direct reading of the contextual attention weights already computed during inference (short-term temporal, at zero additional cost), and a gradient rollback through the recurrent hidden states (long-term temporal). Because the detector is frozen, detection performance is preserved exactly (Delta AUC = 0, verified empirically to ten decimal places). On the UCI Message benchmark, our trained AddGraph baseline reaches an average per-snapshot AUC of 0.8705, exceeding the originally published result; X-AddGraph reproduces every score identically while adding explanations where none existed. Evaluated across four edge populations - confident true positives, low-confidence true positives, false positives, and random samples - the long-term attribution identifies historical snapshots carrying significantly more counterfactual signal than random selection (0.127 vs. 0.074), a capability that no spatially-blind explainer can provide. We release our implementation for full reproducibility.

</details>


### [125] [Personalized Scorer Modeling: A Learning-Based Framework for Deriving Robust Sleep Stage Labels from Multiple Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12446)
*Seyyed Ali Hoseini,Javad Baseri,Hamid Saadatfar,Edris Hoseini Gol,AmirHossein Eshghi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了利用多评分者数据集构建更可靠的睡眠分期参考标注问题，提出了一种基于学习的睡眠图（LBH）方法，通过对每位评分者进行混淆矩阵建模并聚合其标签概率来生成最终参考标注，在两个公开数据集上均优于传统数据集合成睡眠图和最佳评分者方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的自动睡眠分期研究通常只使用单一参考睡眠图进行评估，但已知存在评分者间变异性（inter-scorer variability）。本文旨在探索如何利用多评分者数据集，从多位专家的集体行为中构建更可靠的参考标注，以改善自动分期模型的评估基准。

Method: 使用DOD-H和DOD-O两个公开数据集，提取EEG（C3-M2）和下颌肌电信号的30秒时段，从每种模态提取30个特征（共60个特征）。提出学习型睡眠图（LBH）方法：通过机器学习模型为每位评分者构建混淆矩阵，经列归一化后估计各真实睡眠阶段的概率，再将所有评分者的概率聚合以分配每个时段的最终标签。使用随机森林、支持向量机和多层感知机三种分类器，在仅EEG和EEG+EMG两种设置下进行评价。

Result: LBH方法在整体性能上持续优于数据集睡眠图（DH）和最佳评分者睡眠图（BSH）。最佳结果来自随机森林配合EEG+EMG设置：在DOD-H上达到86.07%准确率、85.46%精确率和85.29% F1分数；在DOD-O上达到86.04%准确率、85.21%精确率和84.70% F1分数。

Conclusion: 个性化评分者建模能够在不丢弃任何个体专家信息的情况下改进参考睡眠图的构建，为自动睡眠分期提供更可靠的评估基准。

Abstract: Sleep stage classification is important for the diagnosis and management of sleep disorders, yet most automatic staging studies evaluate models against a single reference hypnogram despite known inter-scorer variability. This study investigates whether multi-scored datasets can be used to construct more reliable reference labels from the collective behavior of multiple experts. We use the publicly available DOD-H and DOD-O datasets. EEG (C3-M2) and chin EMG signals were segmented into 30-s epochs, and 30 features were extracted from each modality, yielding 60 features for EEG+EMG. We propose a learning-based hypnogram (LBH) that models the stage-specific behavior of each scorer using confusion matrices derived from machine-learning models. After column normalization, these matrices estimate the probability of each true sleep stage given each scorer's label; probabilities are aggregated across scorers to assign the final label for each epoch. LBH was evaluated with random forest, support vector machine, and multilayer perceptron classifiers under EEG-only and EEG+EMG settings, and compared with the dataset hypnogram (DH) and best-scorer hypnogram (BSH). LBH consistently improved overall performance. The best results were obtained with random forest and EEG+EMG, reaching 86.07% accuracy, 85.46% precision, and 85.29% F1-score on DOD-H, and 86.04% accuracy, 85.21% precision, and 84.70% F1-score on DOD-O. These findings suggest that personalized scorer modeling can improve reference hypnogram construction without discarding information from individual experts.

</details>


### [126] [Geometric and Behavioral Stratification in Transformer Residual Streams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12447)
*Nelson Guda*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现训练后的Transformer模型中存在"预测方向"这一特权锚点，残差流中的变异按与该锚点的距离呈现几何和行为上的分层结构。这种分层在18个模型（7B-120B，稠密和MoE，基础与指令微调）中普遍存在，提供了一个高维计算与线性读出共存的几何解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究揭示Transformer模型会发展出特权基（privileged bases），但此前对坐标轴方向的选择机制理解有限。作者希望探究特权基具体选择何种方向，以及这一方向如何组织残差流中的信息结构，从而理解高维计算如何与线性读出兼容。

Method: 以模型当前预测token的unembedding方向作为"预测方向"锚点，测量残差流各维度相对该锚点的几何与行为属性。将残差空间划分为预测近端（prediction-proximal）和预测远端（prediction-distal）区域，进行方差分析、聚类分析及因果扰动实验（逐层破坏不同方差方向），并在18个不同规模与类型的模型上验证。

Result: 预测方向是一个依赖内容定义的锚点，与主方差轴近乎正交。预测近端区域高度结构化，能聚类相关提示；远端区域平坦且对提示组具有反判别性。干扰近端方差方向导致立即发散和任务框架转换，干扰次要方向则延迟发散但保持框架。远端方向虽逐方向弱对齐读出，却在因果和时间上承载重要功能，行为由方向而非幅度驱动。

Conclusion: 预测方向是一种区别于先前描述坐标轴的特权锚点。残差流围绕该锚点呈窄接口（预测近端）与广阔补充（远端）的组织结构，接口虽窄但在功能上具有决定性，为高维计算与线性读出的共存提供了新的几何解释。

Abstract: Trained transformer models develop privileged bases: coordinate axes whose statistics differ from the rest of the residual stream. But what kind of direction does such a basis select? We investigate the prediction direction, the unembedding direction of the token a model currently predicts, and find that it functions as a content-defined privileged anchor. Measured with respect to this anchor, residual-stream variation is geometrically and behaviorally stratified by proximity to the prediction.
  The stratification holds in all eighteen models tested (dense and mixture-of-experts, 7B-120B, base and instruction-tuned). A narrow, scale-invariant prediction interface concentrates readout-relevant structure, while the vast prediction-distal complement expands with model scale. Because the prediction direction sits nearly orthogonal to the principal variance axes, variance-based analyses recover this organization only partly, and the shortfall grows with prompt heterogeneity.
  Anchoring reveals a steep geometric gradient: prediction-proximal regions are highly structured and cluster related prompts, while the complement is flatter and anti-discriminates among prompt groups. The interface is a narrow slice but functionally decisive. Disrupting the variance directions closest to the prediction causes immediate divergence and frequent task-frame shifts; disrupting the next level down delays divergence and preserves framing. The complement is weakly readout-aligned per direction yet causally and temporally load-bearing, and behavior is driven by direction rather than magnitude.
  These results establish the prediction direction as a privileged anchor distinct from previously described coordinate axes, and give a geometric account of how high-dimensional computation coexists with linear readout.

</details>


### [127] [Exemplar-based objective classification of gust-induced loads across multiple flight conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12448)
*Paolo Olivucci,Kowshik Srivatsan,David E. Rival*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于机器学习表示和摘要选择的客观分类方法，用于对飞行器阵风诱导载荷进行分类。该方�对3480个压力载荷测量数据进行编码，选出最小的高显著性代表样本，最终识别出跨越六个飞行姿态的九种基本响应类型，为物理机制提供洞察。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于粗糙参数（如飞行姿态）的标签分类虽可解释但不客观，作者希望找到一种客观的、同时保持可解释性的分类准则，以组织跨多个飞行条件下阵风诱导载荷的复杂性。

Method: 通过机器学习学习表征对大量实验观测进行编码，再应用摘要（summarization）程序选取最小的高显著性代表性样本（exemplars），这些代表性样本为所有观测提供基于相似性的客观分类准则。

Result: 在包含3480个阵风诱导压力载荷测量、覆盖六个飞行姿态的飞翼模型数据库上，识别出九种跨多个姿态反复出现的基本响应类型。对类型瞬态响应的分析揭示了底层流体力学机制的物理直觉。

Conclusion: 该方法能够在保持可解释性的同时提供客观分类准则，选出的代表样本便于专家检验并可用于更精细的实验设计，为阵风载荷研究提供了新的分类框架。

Abstract: Is it possible to find an objective classification criterion that organizes the complexity of gust-induced loads across many flight conditions? And one that remains as interpretable as a labelling based on coarse parameters, such as the flight attitude? Our approach encodes a large number of experimental observations through a machine-learned representation and applies a summarization procedure to select a minimal subset of highly significant exemplars. The exemplars provide a similarity-based objective classification criterion of all the observations, they can be more conveniently inspected by experts and can become subject of more refined experiments. We demonstrate the approach on a database of 3480 pressure-load measurements induced by random gusts on a flying-wing model across six flight attitudes. We find nine fundamental response types that recur across multiple attitudes; analysis of a type's transient response enables physical intuition into the underlying fluid mechanics.

</details>


### [128] [Learning Under Treatment-Induced Label Indeterminacy with Expert Annotations of Counterfactual Outcomes: A Case Study in Neurological Prognostication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12477)
*Xiaobin Shen,Chloe Y. H. Huang,Jonathan Elmer,George H. Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对治疗决策导致临床结局不可观测的预测模型评估问题，提出将评估显式区分为"确定案例"和"不确定案例"的框架，并以心脏骤停后神经预后预测为案例（2,497名患者，其中1,429名因治疗决策导致结局不确定）进行验证。研究发现，在确定案例AUROC相近的模型，其Brier分数和不确定案例概率估计可能差异显著，且提升不确定案例对齐度通常以牺牲确定案例准确性为代价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统临床预测模型假设每个患者的结局都能被清晰观测，但现实中治疗决策往往使临床相关的结局变得永久不可观测。这导致标准评估指标无法捕捉到对最需要预后支持的患者的预测失误，需要显式区分处理两类患者来评估模型。

Method: 提出一个将模型评估显式拆分为确定案例和不确定案例两部分的框架；利用独立临床专家对不确定案例的反事实结局猜测作为目标标签；提出一种能够同时利用两类案例的标签并可调节两者权衡的简单预测模型（基于神经网络及表格基线模型对比）。

Result: 在提出模型与多种表格基线模型中，确定案例AUROC相似的模型在确定案例Brier分数和不确定案例概率估计上可存在显著差异；提升与不确定案例目标标签的对齐度通常以降低确定案例准确性为代价，揭示了传统评估方式所掩盖的显式权衡。

Conclusion: 当治疗决策决定了临床有意义结局是否可观测时，传统评估指标可能遗漏对最需要预后支持的患者的重大失败模式。因此，预测模型评估必须显式考虑确定与不确定案例的差异化处理。

Abstract: Clinical prediction models are often developed as if the outcome of interest were cleanly observed for every patient. This assumption fails when treatment decisions make the clinically relevant outcome permanently unobservable. As a case study of this problem, we consider post-cardiac-arrest neurological prognostication using a cohort of 2,497 patients, including 1,429 patients whose outcomes were rendered indeterminate by treatment decisions. These patients with indeterminate outcomes were reviewed by independent clinical experts, who provided their guesses of counterfactual outcomes about what would have happened to the patients. We refer to these patients as uncertain cases. We also have patients for whom we observe their clinically relevant outcomes; we refer to these patients as certain cases. We propose a framework for evaluating prediction models that explicitly splits the evaluation between certain and uncertain cases. Here, we cannot easily evaluate both types of cases in a uniform manner as the available target labels differ. We then propose a simple prediction model that uses target labels from both certain and uncertain cases in a manner that allows us to trade off between them. Across the proposed neural model and a collection of tabular baselines, models with similar certain-case AUROC can nevertheless differ substantially in both certain-case Brier score and their probability estimates for uncertain cases. Improving alignment with target labels of uncertain cases for our proposed model generally comes at the cost of worse accuracy on certain cases, highlighting an explicit tradeoff that standard evaluation conceals. These results show that when treatment decisions determine whether clinically meaningful outcomes remain observable, conventional evaluation metrics can miss important failure modes in the very patients for whom prognostic support matters most.

</details>


### [129] [When Can You Trust Offline Evaluation of Equal-Cost Top-k Allocation? A Controlled, Reproducible Benchmark and Practitioner's Guide](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12489)
*Binshuang Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对离线策略评估（OPE）中的确定性top-k目标策略进行了系统基准测试，使用六个估计器、五个数据集和两个已知效应扫描，并通过非模拟配对参考验证机制。研究发现三个关键问题：弱重叠主要由logger与目标动作的对齐程度决定而非logger锐度本身；交叉拟合结果误差无法解决优化器诅咒，需要诚实的策略级分割；倾向得分估计误差是最大的性能退化来源（对IPS影响最大，对DR几乎无影响）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组织在预算约束下需要决定对谁进行治疗，并希望在实际部署前了解目标规则（确定性top-k策略）的预期收益。但由于top-k策略消除了所有动作的加权平均，弱重叠问题会直接影响估计，现有OPE方法在此场景下的可靠性和失败机制尚不清楚。

Method: 对六个估计器在五个数据集和两个已知效应扫描下进行基准测试，利用非模拟配对参考验证机制。分析弱重叠的影响因素（logger与目标的动作对齐、logger锐度），检验交叉拟合对优化器诅咒的效果，评估倾向得分估计误差对不同估计器的影响，并对比策略级分割与全样本策略的交叉拟合。

Result: 弱重叠由logger-目标动作对齐主导而非logger锐度；有效样本量可在日志环境间排序风险但无法在单个日志内排序候选且截断点不可迁移。交叉拟合结果误差无法解决重用偏差甚至使其恶化。倾向得分估计误差是最大退化因素，对IPS损伤最大，对DR几乎无影响。倾向得分下限0.02使两个混合估计器退化为其未调优版本。

Conclusion: 在确定性top-k策略的OPE中，动作对齐比logger锐度更关键；诚实的策略级分割（改变估计目标）而非交叉拟合才是解决重用偏差的方式；倾向得分估计误差是最大的实际威胁，需要特别注意。研究发布了基准测试（仅公开数据）。

Abstract: Organizations decide whom to treat under a budget and want to know what a targeting rule would have earned before deploying it. Off-policy evaluation promises this from logged data, but the deployable rule is a deterministic top-k policy: it removes all averaging over actions, so weak overlap hits the estimate directly. We benchmark six estimators across five datasets and two known-effect sweeps, and validate the mechanisms against a non-simulated paired reference. First, weak overlap is governed by logger-target action alignment, not by logging sharpness alone: what governs support is the logger's probability of the target's actions. Sharpening a logger built from the target's own score barely moves overlap over the tested range; action-level disagreement collapses it. Effective sample size ranks this risk across logging environments, but is weak at ranking candidates within the single log a practitioner holds, and its cut point does not transfer. Second, the optimizer's curse is not fixed by cross-fitting the outcome nuisance. When the rule is fit on the data used to evaluate it, cross-fitting the nuisance alone leaves the reuse bias in place and makes it worse. Honest policy-level splitting avoids the reuse by targeting the learning procedure's value -- a change of estimand, not a de-biasing of the full-sample policy. Third, propensity-estimation error is the largest degradation we measure: an out-of-fold estimate hurts IPS more than any other stress we apply, leaves doubly-robust estimation almost unchanged, and can invert the overlap diagnostic itself. Logging is synthesized and propensities floored at 0.02, so every failure occurs with bounded weights; the floor also reduces the two tuned hybrids to their untuned parents, leaving four practically distinct estimators, and all exact-value surfaces are synthetic or semi-synthetic. We release the benchmark; public data only.

</details>


### [130] [Exploring Oversmoothing with Householder Matrices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12514)
*Bhaskar Karol*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了HouseGNN（Householder图神经网络），通过使用Householder反射器结合GroupSort激活来替代标准GCN的隐藏状态更新方式，解决深度图神经网络中的过度平滑问题，并证明了其规范保持、尺度和符号不变性以及节点间距离可变化等核心性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度图神经网络存在过度平滑问题——随着网络深度增加，由于归一化图传播算子被反复直接应用于隐藏表示，节点表示会逐渐坍缩到低信息子空间。传统GCN方法无法有效解决这一问题。

Method: HouseGNN不使用标准GCN的隐藏状态更新方式，而是仅用聚合的邻居消息来估计反射方向；节点嵌入通过Householder反射器后接GroupSort激活来更新，形成逐段正交层，在每个节点和每个深度都保持欧几里得范数。

Result: 论文证明了三个核心性质：(i)每个内部层都保持逐节点欧几里得范数；(ii)Householder反射器在消息中具有尺度不变性和符号不变性；(iii)节点对之间的成对距离可以通过节点级正交算子之间的不匹配而发生变化。

Conclusion: HouseGNN通过设计逐段正交的层结构，在理论上保证了信息在深层网络中不会因过度平滑而丢失，为解决深度GNN的过度平滑问题提供了新的理论框架和方法。

Abstract: Deep graph neural networks(GNNs) suffer from oversmoothing- a progressive collapse of node representation towards a low information subspace as network depth increases because the normalized graph propagation operator is repeatedly applied directly to the hidden representations. In this work we study Householder Graph Neural Network (HouseGNN). Rather than updating the hidden state like standard GCN, HouseGNN uses the aggregated neighbourhood message solely to estimate a reflection direction; the node embedding is then updated by a Householder reflector followed by GroupSort, yielding a piecewise orthogonal layer that preserves Euclidean norm at every node and at every depth. We prove three core properties: (i) every internal layer preserves the node-wise Euclidean norm; (ii) the Householder reflector is scale scale and sign-invariant in the message; and (iii) pairwise distance between nodes can change through mismatch between node-wise orthogonal operators.

</details>


### [131] [GENADA: efficient generative time series adversarial attack framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12535)
*Michael Baronov,Denis Vorobev,Margarita Rusanova,Petr Sokerin,Alexey Zaytsev*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出GENADA（生成式对抗攻击），通过学习生成模型在单次前向传播中直接生成欺骗性扰动，替代计算开销高昂的迭代梯度攻击方法，在时间序列领域的多个神经模型和数据集上实现了与强基线相当的攻击质量，同时显著降低了推理阶段的扰动生成时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度模型在时间序列分析中广泛使用但易受对抗攻击。现有的梯度攻击方法（迭代一阶方法）需要在每次迭代中反复反向传播计算输入梯度，计算负担沉重。因此需要一种更高效的方法来生成对抗扰动。

Method: 提出GENADA（生成式对抗攻击），训练一个生成模型来直接产生欺骗性扰动，仅需单次前向传播即可生成攻击样本。包含单步和迭代两种生成攻击方案，并设计了相应的训练流程。

Result: 在时间序列领域的多个神经模型和数据集上进行验证（包括受控的低维设置），实验结果表明GENADA能够达到与强基线相当的攻击质量，同时在推理时生成扰动所需的时间更少。

Conclusion: 生成式对抗攻击是一种高效的对抗攻击替代方案，在保持攻击效果的同时显著降低了计算开销，为时间序列模型的鲁棒性评估提供了更实用的工具。

Abstract: Deep learning models are widely used for time series analysis in domains such as healthcare, finance, energy systems, and environmental monitoring. However, these models remain vulnerable to adversarial attacks, where small input perturbations cause severe degradation in predictive performance. Commonly used gradient-based attacks, iterative first-order methods, are computationally burdensome, as they repeatedly backpropagate through the victim model to compute input gradients during a number of iterative refinement steps. We propose a GENerative ADversarial Attack (GENADA) that learns a generative model to produce deceptive perturbations directly in a single forward pass and a procedure to train it. Variants include single-step and iterative generative attack schemes. The validation considers attacks on several neural models and datasets in the time-series domain, a controlled, low-dimensional setting. Empirically, GENADA achieves comparable attack quality to strong baselines while requiring less time to generate perturbations during inference.

</details>


### [132] [Scaling Automatic Research Agents via World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12564)
*Xiyuan Yang,Sheikh Sarwar,Jingru Cheng,Zhan Shi,Duanshun Li,Huiyuan Chen,Haiyang Zhang,Chenlei Guo,Jingrui He,Zhenyu Liao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出World Model RL（WMRL）方法，用世界模型替代AutoResearch智能体强化学习训练中的环境执行环节，解决环境执行成为训练瓶颈的问题。通过在线去偏和逆方差去噪两种缓解策略处理世界模型的不完美性，在多个任务上实现3-4倍加速，且4B/9B模型性能超越48B/120B的大模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在AutoResearch智能体的强化学习训练中，智能体生成和环境执行两个环节的扩展方式根本不同：生成可通过批处理共享计算，而每个执行需要独占沙箱和真实机器时间。因此环境执行主导训练成本，成为轨迹增长时的瓶颈。如何消除这一瓶颈是本文的核心动机。

Method: 提出WMRL框架，用世界模型替代环境执行以消除训练瓶颈。由于世界模型可能存在不完美性，其奖励会受到偏差和噪声污染，因此设计了两种缓解机制：在线去偏（Online Debiasing）用于抵消偏差，逆方差去噪（Inverse-Variance Denoising）用于抑制噪声。理论上证明了两种缓解机制能够严格改善收敛保证。

Result: WMRL在各种任务和不同智能体规模下实现3-4倍训练加速，同时性能超过标准RL基线。后训练的4B和9B智能体在保留基准上超越48B和120B的开权重模型。此外，WMRL还成功迁移到具身VLA策略的后训练中，验证了方法的泛化性。

Conclusion: WMRL通过世界模型替代环境执行，有效解决了AutoResearch智能体RL训练中的扩展瓶颈问题。在线去偏和逆方差去噪两种策略确保了世界模型不完美情况下训练的稳定性与性能。该方法不仅适用于AutoResearch场景，还能泛化到具身VLA策略等更广泛的后训练任务中。

Abstract: Automating empirical research is a long-standing direction of AI. Recent automatic research (AutoResearch) agents bring this goal within reach, as modern LLMs show the capability to independently implement solutions and learn from the execution outcomes. Behind these gains, post-training (especially RL) plays a central role. In this paper, we identify a fundamental tension when scaling RL for these agents: the two components of every AutoResearch trajectory (agent generation and environment execution) scale in very different manners, since all generation shares compute through batching, while each execution occupies its exclusive sandbox and real machine time. As a result, the environment execution dominates the training cost and becomes the bottleneck as trajectories grow. To resolve this tension, we propose World Model RL (WMRL), which replaces environment execution with a world model to remove this bottleneck. Additionally, the world model can be imperfect, as its rewards are corrupted by bias and noise. Therefore, we further equip WMRL with two mitigations, Online Debiasing and Inverse-Variance Denoising, which offset the bias and suppress the noise respectively. Theoretically, we prove that both mitigations of WMRL strictly improve the convergence guarantee. Empirically, WMRL accelerates training by 3-4x on various tasks at different agent scales, while exceeding the performance of standard RL baselines. Moreover, our post-trained 4B and 9B agents outperform much larger open-weight agents of 48B and 120B on held-out benchmarks. Beyond AutoResearch, WMRL also transfers to post-training embodied VLA policies, which demonstrates the generalizability of our method.

</details>


### [133] [Prof-K: Probabilistic One-Pass Filtering for Efficient Top-k Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12573)
*Tadeusz Dziarmaga,Witold Sikora,Łukasz Struski,Jacek Tabor,Marcin Mazur*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Prof-K，一种快速、可扩展且与数据分布无关的top-k选择算法，通过单遍过滤流程（随机采样估计自适应阈值 + 流式缓冲 + 精确top-k），提供概率正确性保证，在PyTorch topk和RadiK基础上实现1.5x-10x加速，并可应用于稀疏自编码器训练等场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着数据规模增长，现有top-k方法效率低下：精确方法计算和内存开销过高，近似方法依赖脆弱的启发式规则，在对抗性或重尾数据下性能退化，缺乏稳健性保证。

Method: Prof-K采用单遍过滤机制：先用小规模随机采样估计自适应阈值，然后将N个输入元素流式写入紧凑缓冲区，最后在缓冲区上运行精确top-k程序，以至少1-ε的概率恢复真实top-k元素。推导了正确性和缓冲区大小的概率保证，并给出近似最优采样大小。

Result: 实验表明Prof-K相比高度优化的PyTorch topk和RadiK实现获得1.5x-10x加速，在大规模、小到中等k值场景下增益最大；通过放宽召回目标可提供精度-速度权衡；在BatchTopK稀疏自编码器训练中展示了实际应用价值。

Conclusion: Prof-K提供了与输入分布无关的健壮top-k算法，具备概率正确性保证和可调精度-速度权衡，在无需依赖具体数据分布假设的前提下实现了显著性能提升，是对现有top-k方法的重要改进。

Abstract: Top-k selection is a fundamental computational primitive with applications spanning databases, information retrieval, signal processing, and modern machine learning workloads, including sparse activations and attention pruning. As data sizes grow, existing approaches become inefficient: exact methods incur high memory and compute overhead, while approximate methods often rely on brittle heuristics that degrade under adversarial or heavy-tailed inputs. In this paper, we introduce Prof-K, a fast, scalable, and distribution-agnostic top-k algorithm with probabilistic correctness guarantees. Prof-K performs a single-pass filtering procedure: a small random sample estimates an adaptive threshold, the N input elements are streamed once into a compact buffer, and an exact top-k routine on this buffer recovers the true top-k elements with probability at least 1 - $ε$, where $ε$ > 0 is user specified. We derive high-probability guarantees for correctness and buffer size, together with an approximately optimal sample size that minimizes overhead as a function of N and k. Empirically, Prof-K achieves 1.5x-10x speedups over the highly optimized PyTorch topk and recent RadiK implementations, with the largest gains in the large-scale, small-to-moderate-k regime where prior methods struggle most. Unlike previous approaches, these guarantees hold independently of the input distribution, ensuring robustness to adversarial settings. By relaxing the recall target (e.g., recovering 95% of the true top-k values), Prof-K additionally provides a principled accuracy-speed trade-off. We further demonstrate its impact on training BatchTopK Sparse Autoencoders (SAEs), where top-k selection constitutes a significant portion of the training cost.

</details>


### [134] [Represent, Then Generate: Multimodal-Conditioned Time-Series Generation under Irregular Missingness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12592)
*Haochen Zhang,Jiaheng Guo,Yu-Chao Huang,Nicholas Knoz,Tianlong Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ReCoGen是一个两阶段条件生成框架，用于从不完整的多模态生理信号中合成缺失的目标信号（如连续血糖或动脉血压），通过逐模态掩码自编码器提取紧凑token，再以流匹配生成器融合静态条件，在三个基准上超越六种基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床监测中许多最有信息量的生理信号是侵入性、昂贵或对特定患者不可用的。现有的条件生成器仅针对单一条件模态设计，无法处理实际临床中异质性、不规则缺失的时间序列信号与静态变量的混合情况。

Method: 两阶段框架：阶段I为每个模态训练一个掩码自编码器，将时变条件信号蒸馏为紧凑且对缺失鲁棒的token序列；阶段II训练一个流匹配生成器，通过可学习的跨注意力机制融合各模态token，并结合双路径（token+AdaLN）处理静态条件，最终生成目标信号。

Result: 在AI-READI连续血糖监测和MIMIC-III/MIMIC-IV动脉血压生成等三个生理基准上，ReCoGen在所有16个（数据集、任务、指标）设置中获得最佳下游效用，超越六种代表性条件生成器；其中13个设置的效用达到或超过真实信号的效用。

Conclusion: ReCoGen通过解耦多模态条件表示与目标生成，有效将常规采集的临床信号转化为侵入性或不可用信号的替代品，为迈向更少侵入、更低成本的连续临床监测迈出了一步。

Abstract: Continuous physiological time series underpin modern clinical monitoring, yet many of the most informative signals are invasive, expensive, or simply unavailable for a given patient. Conditional generation offers a remedy: an absent signal can be synthesized from co-recorded signals and routine clinical variables. Existing generators, however, are built around a single conditioning modality and degrade when forced to handle the heterogeneous, irregularly missing mix of time-variant signals and static covariates seen in practice. We propose ReCoGen (Represent Conditions, then Generate), a two-stage framework that decouples multimodal condition representation from target generation. Stage I trains one masked autoencoder per modality, distilling each time-variant condition into a compact and missingness-tolerant token sequence. Stage II trains a flow-matching generator that fuses these tokens with static conditions to synthesize the target signal. Across three physiological benchmarks, including continuous glucose monitoring on AI-READI and arterial blood pressure generation on MIMIC-III and MIMIC-IV, ReCoGen attains the best downstream utility on all sixteen (dataset, task, metric) settings, surpassing six representative conditional generators; on thirteen of them its utility also reaches or exceeds the utility measured on the real signal, a reference we read as an approximate anchor rather than a ceiling. Ablations trace the gains to the conditioning path: learnable cross-attention over the frozen per-modality encoders, and a dual token-plus-AdaLN route for the static conditions. ReCoGen thus turns routinely collected signals into informative surrogates for invasive or unavailable ones, a step toward less invasive, lower-cost continuous clinical monitoring.

</details>


### [135] [Predicting When Random Low-Dimensional Reparameterizations Train Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12597)
*Andrew Cheng,Ali Eslamian,Jie Cheng,Mehdi Zargham,Qiang Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了通过随机低维重参数化训练神经网络的可行性，提出了方向分辨的二次主公式来预测达到低损失区域所需的最小潜在空间维度，并基于此设计了Random Mapping Networks (RaMaN)架构，通过结构化Hadamard或种子再生成的高斯映射避免全尺寸随机映射的存储开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经网络可以通过随机低维重参数化进行训练或微调，但一个关键的实际问题是：潜在搜索空间必须多大才能到达低损失区域？这个问题对训练效率和存储开销都有重要影响，需要理论分析来指导设计。

Method: 首先将已知的可达性转变表达为等价的锥形式，以极锥的统计维度为中心（针对紧凑凸目标）。提出方向分辨的二次主公式，从曲率谱和参考到解的位移分布预测随机切片残差。基于此公式推导自洽的各向同性方向预测器，并在保守的半径特化版本中恢复早期的高斯宽度二次界。设计RaMaN框架，使用结构化Hadamard或种子再生成高斯映射实例化预测的潜在维度，避免O(dP)存储，将优化器状态内存从O(P)降至O(d)。同时开发无矩阵曲率近似和无需扫描的维度选择方法。

Result: 在受控二次实验和神经曲率实验中，方向分辨预测器紧密跟踪实测转变位置，在位移方向重要时优于与方向无关的近似方法。端到端实验在图像和语言模型上展现出清晰且依赖协议的训练转变。

Conclusion: 本文提供了从理论到实践完整的随机低维重参数化分析框架，证明了方向分辨的曲率信息是预测潜在空间所需维度的关键，同时RaMaN架构在保持理论预测性能的同时大幅降低了存储开销。

Abstract: Neural networks can often be trained or fine-tuned through random low-dimensional reparameterization, where a small latent vector is mapped into a full parameter update by a frozen random map. This raises a practical question: how large must the latent search space be to reach a low-loss region? We first express the known accessibility transition in an equivalent conic form, centered for compact convex targets at the statistical dimension of the polar cone. Our main theoretical contribution is an orientation-resolved quadratic master formula that predicts the random-slice residual from both the curvature spectrum and the reference-to-solution displacement profile. It yields a self-consistent isotropic-orientation predictor and, in a conservative radius-only specialization, recovers the earlier Gaussian-width quadratic bound. Building on this analysis, we introduce Random Mapping Networks (RaMaN), which instantiate the predicted latent dimension using structured Hadamard or seed-regenerated Gaussian maps. These constructions avoid the O(dP) storage of dense random maps and reduce optimizer-state memory from O(P) to O(d). We also develop matrix-free curvature approximations and sweep-free dimension selection. Across controlled quadratic and neural-curvature experiments, the orientation-resolved predictor closely tracks measured transition locations and outperforms orientation-agnostic approximations when displacement direction matters. End-to-end experiments further show sharp, protocol-dependent training transitions across image and language models.

</details>


### [136] [The Boolean Power of ReLU](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12617)
*Pablo Barceló,Floris Geerts,Matthias Lanzinger,Klara Pakhomenko,Jan Van den Bussche*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明了在带布尔节点特征的有限简单无向图上，对于任何由最终常量激活函数组成的集合Σ，Σ-MPLang能表达的布尔查询严格属于ReLU-MPLang可表达布尔查询的真子类，从而解决了ReLU-MPLang是否比trReLU-MPLang在布尔查询上更强大的开放问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前有开放问题悬而未决：ReLU-MPLang是否比trReLU-MPLang在布尔查询表达能力上更强大。本文旨在严格解决这一问题。

Method: 通过构造严格包含关系来证明：对任意由最终常量激活函数组成的集合Σ，Σ-MPLang所表达的布尔查询类严格包含于ReLU-MPLang所表达的布尔查询类。

Result: 证明了Σ-MPLang布尔查询类是ReLU-MPLang布尔查询类的严格子类；作为推论，ReLU-GNN在布尔特征图上的布尔查询表达能力严格强于{TrReLU,id}-GNN。

Conclusion: ReLU激活函数在布尔查询表达能力上具有不可替代的优势，其表达能力严格超越由最终常量激活函数构成的任意MPLang变体，这为图神经网络表达能力理论研究提供了新的严格结果。

Abstract: We prove that, on finite simple undirected graphs equipped with a single Boolean node feature, the Boolean queries expressible in $Σ$-MPLang, for any collection $Σ$ of eventually constant activation functions and with arbitrary real coefficients, form a strict subclass of the Boolean queries expressible in ReLU-MPLang. We thereby settle a recently posed open problem: whether ReLU-MPLang is more powerful than trReLU-MPLang when it comes to Boolean queries. In particular, this implies that ReLU-GNNs are strictly more expressive than {TrReLU,id}-GNNs with respect to Boolean queries on Boolean-featured graphs.

</details>


### [137] [Structure-preserving uncertainty quantification for GENERIC dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12624)
*Zequn He,Celia Reina*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出结构保持认知神经网络（S-PENNs），一种针对具有硬架构约束的科学机器学习模型的不确定性量化通用框架。通过将轻量级epinets附加到预训练模型的约束组件上，确保所有采样实现都满足物理可容许性条件，并结合分裂共形预测提供具有有限样本边际覆盖保证的预测区间。在GENERIC动力学三个数值实验中验证，计算成本比深度集成降低1-3个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的不确定性量化方法在处理具有硬物理约束的科学机器学习模型时存在局限：标准UQ方法可能违反编码的可容许性条件，需要修改架构，或产生高昂计算成本。因此需要一种既能保持物理结构约束又能高效量化不确定性的通用框架。

Method: 提出S-PENNs框架：将轻量级epinets附加到预训练模型的约束组件上，使每个采样实现都保持物理结构约束；结合GENERIC动力学实现热力学一致性滚动（保持第一和第二定律）；使用分裂共形预测作为事后校准方法，提供有限样本边际覆盖保证的预测区间。

Result: 在三个数值示例（谐波振荡器耦合热浴、理想化化学马达、一维粘塑性模型）中，S-PENNs产生热力学一致且校准良好的预测区间，计算成本比深度集成降低约1到3个数量级。

Conclusion: S-PENNs为具有硬约束的科学机器学习模型提供了一种高效、可扩展的UQ框架，虽聚焦于GENERIC动力学，但可推广到计算力学中具有硬或软约束的更广泛的科学机器学习模型。

Abstract: Structure-preserving machine learning embeds physical structure directly into model architectures, yet uncertainty quantification (UQ) for such hard-constrained models remains limited because standard UQ methods may violate the encoded admissibility conditions, require architectural modifications, or impose substantial computational costs. In this work, we propose Structure-Preserving Epistemic Neural Networks (S-PENNs), a general framework for UQ in scientific machine learning models with hard architectural constraints, and instantiate it for GENERIC (General Equation for Non-Equilibrium Reversible-Irreversible Coupling) dynamics. S-PENNs preserve the structural constraints of a pretrained model by attaching lightweight epinets to its constrained components, ensuring that every sampled realization remains physically admissible by construction. When applied to GENERIC dynamics, such a proposed framework yields thermodynamically consistent rollouts that preserve the first and second laws.
